/// Logic
component SignalActive
{
IDs_Appear = "";
IDs_AppearTooltip = "Triggers specified objects to APPEAR. Use space to separate multiple IDs (e.g. 1 2 3)";
HideAppearOnStart = false;
HideAppearOnStartTooltip = "If true, objects in IDs_Appear will be hidden when the map loads.";


IDs_Disappear = "";
IDs_DisappearTooltip = "Triggers specified objects to DISAPPEAR. Use space to separate multiple IDs (e.g. 4 5 6)";


IDs_Toggle = "";
IDs_ToggleTooltip = "TOGGLES visibility of specified objects. Use space to separate multiple IDs (e.g. 7 8 9)";


NetworkSync = false;
NetworkSyncTooltip = "Enable this if visibility should be synced across clients. Networked also has to be enabled.";


TriggerLimit = 0;
TriggerLimitTooltip = "The maximum times the component can be triggered. No limit if set to 0.";


_targetObjectsAppear = List();
_targetObjectsDisappear = List();
_targetObjectsToggle = List();
_triggerCount = 0;


function OnGameStart()
{
var ids1 = String.Split(self.IDs_Appear, " ");
for (i in Range(0, ids1.Count, 1))
{
var id1 = ids1.Get(i);
if (id1 != "")
{
var obj1 = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id1));
if (obj1 != null) 
{ 
if (self.HideAppearOnStart == true) { obj1.Active = false; }
self._targetObjectsAppear.Add(obj1); 
}
}
}
var ids2 = String.Split(self.IDs_Disappear, " ");
for (j in Range(0, ids2.Count, 1))
{
var id2 = ids2.Get(j);
if (id2 != "")
{
var obj2 = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id2));
if (obj2 != null) { self._targetObjectsDisappear.Add(obj2); }
}
}
var ids3 = String.Split(self.IDs_Toggle, " ");
for (k in Range(0, ids3.Count, 1))
{
var id3 = ids3.Get(k);
if (id3 != "")
{
var obj3 = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id3));
if (obj3 != null) { self._targetObjectsToggle.Add(obj3); }
}
}
}


function OnNetworkMessage(sender, message)
{
var data = Json.LoadFromString(message);
self._triggerCount = data.Get("triggerCount");
var states = data.Get("states");
var idx = 0;
for (m in Range(0, self._targetObjectsAppear.Count, 1))
{
self._targetObjectsAppear.Get(m).Active = states.Get(idx);
idx = idx + 1;
}
for (n in Range(0, self._targetObjectsDisappear.Count, 1))
{
self._targetObjectsDisappear.Get(n).Active = states.Get(idx);
idx = idx + 1;
}
for (p in Range(0, self._targetObjectsToggle.Count, 1))
{
self._targetObjectsToggle.Get(p).Active = states.Get(idx);
idx = idx + 1;
}
}


function OnPlayerJoin(player)
{
if (self.NetworkSync == true)
{
if (self.NetworkView.Owner == Network.MyPlayer)
{
self.NetworkView.SendMessage(player, self.CreateSyncMessage());
}
}
}


function OnCollisionEnter(other)
{
if (other.Type == "Human")
{
if (other.IsMine == true)
{
if (self.TriggerLimit != 0)
{
if (self._triggerCount >= self.TriggerLimit) { return; }
}
self._triggerCount = self._triggerCount + 1;
self.RunAction("Appear", self._targetObjectsAppear);
self.RunAction("Disappear", self._targetObjectsDisappear);
self.RunAction("Toggle", self._targetObjectsToggle);
if (self.NetworkSync == true)
{
self.NetworkView.SendMessageAll(self.CreateSyncMessage());
}
}
}
}


function RunAction(action, targets)
{
for (q in Range(0, targets.Count, 1))
{
var targetObj = targets.Get(q);
if (action == "Disappear") { targetObj.Active = false; }
if (action == "Appear") { targetObj.Active = true; }
if (action == "Toggle") { targetObj.Active = !targetObj.Active; }
}
}

function CreateSyncMessage()
{
var message = Dict();
var statesList = List();
for (r in Range(0, self._targetObjectsAppear.Count, 1)) { statesList.Add(self._targetObjectsAppear.Get(r).Active); }
for (s in Range(0, self._targetObjectsDisappear.Count, 1)) { statesList.Add(self._targetObjectsDisappear.Get(s).Active); }
for (t in Range(0, self._targetObjectsToggle.Count, 1)) { statesList.Add(self._targetObjectsToggle.Get(t).Active); }
message.Set("states", statesList);
message.Set("triggerCount", self._triggerCount);
var result = Json.SaveToString(message);
return result;
}
}
component Egg
{
    _Touched = false;
    
    CustomEggText = "";
    CustomFontColor = "";
    CustomFontSize = 0;


    ObjectsToShow = "";
    ObjectsToHide = "";


    CustomEggTextTooltip = "Overrides the default EggText of the EggHandler for this specific egg.";
    CustomFontColorTooltip = "Overrides the default EggFontColor for this specific egg.";
    CustomFontSizeTooltip = "Overrides the default EggFontSize for this specific egg.";
    
    ObjectsToShowTooltip = "Enter multiple MapObject IDs separated by space (e.g., 10 11 12) to SHOW when touched. These are hidden on start.";
    ObjectsToHideTooltip = "Enter multiple MapObject IDs separated by space (e.g., 13 14 15) to HIDE when touched. These reappear on reset.";


    function Init()
    {
    }


    function ResetStatus()
    {
        self._Touched = false;
        self.MapObject.Active = true;


        idsS = String.Split(self.ObjectsToShow, " ");
        for (i in Range(0, idsS.Count, 1)) {
            id = idsS.Get(i);
            if (id != "" && id != " ") {
                obj = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id));
                if (obj != null) { obj.Active = false; }
            }
        }
        
        idsH = String.Split(self.ObjectsToHide, " ");
        for (i in Range(0, idsH.Count, 1)) {
            id = idsH.Get(i);
            if (id != "" && id != " ") {
                obj = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id));
                if (obj != null) { obj.Active = true; }
            }
        }
    }


    function OnCollisionEnter(other) 
    {
        if (other.Type == "Human" && other.IsMine && !self._Touched)
        {
            self._Touched = true;


            idsS = String.Split(self.ObjectsToShow, " ");
            for (i in Range(0, idsS.Count, 1)) {
                id = idsS.Get(i);
                if (id != "" && id != " ") {
                    obj = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id));
                    if (obj != null) { obj.Active = true; }
                }
            }


            idsH = String.Split(self.ObjectsToHide, " ");
            for (i in Range(0, idsH.Count, 1)) {
                id = idsH.Get(i);
                if (id != "" && id != " ") {
                    obj = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id));
                    if (obj != null) { obj.Active = false; }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}


component EggHandler
{
    Description = "Do not use semicolons (;) to avoid system errors.";


    EggText = "O";
    UseSpace = false;
    EggFontColor = "#FFCC00";
    EggFontSize = "25";
    
    ExtraText = "Eggs to Find∶";
    ExtraFontColor = "White";
    ExtraFontSize = "20";
    ExtraTextPosition = "Above";
    HideExtraTextOnFinish = "Yes";
    
    FinishText = "";
    FinishFontColor = "Red";
    FinishFontSize = "20";


    ObjectsToShow = "";
    ObjectsToHide = "";


    LabelPosition = "MiddleRight";
    PaddingTop = 0;
    PaddingBottom = 0;
    PaddingLeft = 0;    
    PaddingRight = 0;


    ShowServerMessage = true;
    ServerMessage = "has found all the eggs!";


    UseSpaceTooltip = "Add a space between each egg text icon.";
    EggFontColorTooltip = "Use color names or Hex codes like #FFFFFF";
    ExtraTextTooltip = "This text will be displayed near the egg count.";
    ExtraFontColorTooltip = "Use color names or Hex codes like #FFFFFF";
    ExtraTextPositionTooltip = "Choose whether ExtraText appears Above or Below the egg icons.";
    HideExtraTextOnFinishTooltip = "Hide the ExtraText after all eggs are collected.";
    
    FinishTextTooltip = "This message appears only after all eggs are found.";
    FinishFontColorTooltip = "Use color names or Hex codes like #FFFFFF";
    
    ObjectsToShowTooltip = "Enter Object IDs to SHOW after finding all eggs. These will be HIDDEN on game start.";
    ObjectsToHideTooltip = "Enter Object IDs to HIDE after finding all eggs. These will REAPPEAR on reset.";


    LabelPositionTooltip = "Select the UI position on the screen.";
    PaddingTopTooltip = "Push the entire UI down.";
    PaddingBottomTooltip = "Push the entire UI up.";
    PaddingLeftTooltip = "Push the entire UI to the right.";
    PaddingRightTooltip = "Push the entire UI to the left.";


    ExtraTextPositionDropbox = "Above, Below";
    HideExtraTextOnFinishDropbox = "Yes, No";
    LabelPositionDropbox = "TopLeft, TopRight, MiddleLeft, MiddleCenter, MiddleRight, BottomLeft, BottomRight";


    _StaticEggList = List();
    _TotalEggs = 0;
    _Finished = false;
    _Initialized = false;
    _ResetLockFrame = false;


    function Init()
    {
        self._StaticEggList = Map.FindMapObjectsByComponent("Egg");
        self._TotalEggs = self._StaticEggList.Count;
    }


    function ResetAll()
    {
        self._Finished = false;
        self._ResetLockFrame = true;


        for (i in Range(0, self._StaticEggList.Count, 1)) {
            eggObj = self._StaticEggList.Get(i);
            if (eggObj != null) {
                eggObj.Active = true;
                eggComp = eggObj.GetComponent("Egg");
                if (eggComp != null) { eggComp.ResetStatus(); }
            }
        }


        idsH = String.Split(self.ObjectsToHide, " ");
        for (i in Range(0, idsH.Count, 1)) {
            id = idsH.Get(i);
            if (id != "" && id != " ") {
                obj = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id));
                if (obj != null) { obj.Active = true; }
            }
        }


        idsS = String.Split(self.ObjectsToShow, " ");
        for (i in Range(0, idsS.Count, 1)) {
            id = idsS.Get(i);
            if (id != "" && id != " ") {
                obj = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id));
                if (obj != null) { obj.Active = false; }
            }
        }
    }


    function OnTick()
    {
        if (!self._Initialized) {
            self._Initialized = true;
            self.ResetAll();
            return;
        }


        currentCount = 0;
        eggIcons = "";
        for (i in Range(0, self._StaticEggList.Count, 1)) {
            eggObj = self._StaticEggList.Get(i);
            if (eggObj != null && eggObj.Active) {
                eggComp = eggObj.GetComponent("Egg");
                if (eggComp != null && !eggComp._Touched) {
                    currentCount = currentCount + 1;
                    if (eggComp.CustomEggText != "") { tText = eggComp.CustomEggText; } else { tText = self.EggText; }
                    if (eggComp.CustomFontColor != "") { tColor = eggComp.CustomFontColor; } else { tColor = self.EggFontColor; }
                    if (eggComp.CustomFontSize > 0) { tSize = Convert.ToString(eggComp.CustomFontSize); } else { tSize = self.EggFontSize; }
                    styled = "<size=" + tSize + "><color=" + tColor + ">" + tText + "</color></size>";
                    if (self.UseSpace) { eggIcons = eggIcons + styled + " "; } else { eggIcons = eggIcons + styled; }
                }
            }
        }


        if (self._ResetLockFrame) {
            if (currentCount == self._TotalEggs) {
                self._ResetLockFrame = false;
            }
        }


        if (self._TotalEggs > 0 && currentCount == 0 && !self._ResetLockFrame) {
            if (!self._Finished) {
                self._Finished = true;
                
                idsS = String.Split(self.ObjectsToShow, " ");
                for (i in Range(0, idsS.Count, 1)) {
                    id = idsS.Get(i);
                    if (id != "" && id != " ") {
                        obj = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id));
                        if (obj != null) { obj.Active = true; }
                    }
                }
                
                idsH = String.Split(self.ObjectsToHide, " ");
                for (i in Range(0, idsH.Count, 1)) {
                    id = idsH.Get(i);
                    if (id != "" && id != " ") {
                        obj = Map.FindMapObjectByID(Convert.ToInt(id));
                        if (obj != null) { obj.Active = false; }
                    }
                }
                if (self.ShowServerMessage) { Game.PrintAll(Network.MyPlayer.Name + " " + self.ServerMessage); }
            }
        } else {
            if (currentCount > 0) {
                self._Finished = false;
            }
        }


        lSpace = ""; for (i in Range(0, self.PaddingLeft, 1)) { lSpace = lSpace + "  "; }
        rSpace = ""; for (i in Range(0, self.PaddingRight, 1)) { rSpace = rSpace + "  "; }
        tSpace = ""; for (i in Range(0, self.PaddingTop, 1)) { tSpace = tSpace + String.Newline; }
        bSpace = ""; for (i in Range(0, self.PaddingBottom, 1)) { bSpace = bSpace + String.Newline; }
        
        extraS = "";
        if (!(self._Finished && self.HideExtraTextOnFinish == "Yes") && self.ExtraText != "") {
            extraS = "<size=" + self.ExtraFontSize + "><color=" + self.ExtraFontColor + ">" + self.ExtraText + "</color></size>";
        }
        
        content = "";
        if (self.ExtraTextPosition == "Above") {
            if (extraS != "") { content = extraS + String.Newline; }
            content = content + eggIcons;
        } else {
            content = eggIcons;
            if (extraS != "") { content = content + String.Newline + extraS; }
        }
        
        if (self._Finished && self.FinishText != "") {
            content = content + String.Newline + "<size=" + self.FinishFontSize + "><color=" + self.FinishFontColor + ">" + self.FinishText + "</color></size>";
        }
        
        dot = "<color=#00000000>.</color>";
        UI.SetLabel(self.LabelPosition, tSpace + dot + String.Newline + lSpace + content + rSpace + String.Newline + dot + bSpace);
    }
}


component EggResetter
{
    function OnCollisionEnter(other)
    {
        if (other.Type == "Human" && other.IsMine) {
            hObjs = Map.FindMapObjectsByComponent("EggHandler");
            if (hObjs.Count > 0) {
                h = hObjs.Get(0).GetComponent("EggHandler");
                if (h != null) { h.ResetAll(); }
            }
        }
    }
}
